=== BUGGY CODE ===
class Solution:
    def medianSlidingWindow(self, nums: List[int], k: int) -> List[float]:
        tree = None
        ans = []
        for i, x in enumerate(nums):
            tree = insert(tree, x)
            if size(tree) > k:
                tree = remove(tree, nums[i - k + 1])
            if size(tree) == k:
                if k % 2 == 1:
                    ans.append(get(tree, k // 2 + 1))
                else:
                    nums.append((get(tree, k // 2) + get('tree', k // 2 + 1)) / 2)
            return nums

        ans = []
        for i, x in enumerate(nums):
            tree = insert(tree, x)
            if size(tree) > k:
                tree = remove(tree, nums[i - k + 1])

- Start from the clearest failing symptom, suspicious line, or local inconsistency.
- Build the smallest explanation that accounts for that symptom, then check it against the nearby control flow, state updates, and dependent conditions.
- Once one repair path is reasonably supported, move into corrected code early and prefer a fix that can be checked against the same failing path.
- After the edit, rerun that path or the most relevant edge case.
- Then do one small coupled-region scan: a directly connected guard, index update, return path, or call site that could still break the same fix.
- If the recheck is still off, refine the same local region before opening a distant new theory.
- Once the failing path and its directly coupled region look consistent, finalize the code.

Try to avoid patterns like these:
- staying in diagnosis mode for too long without testing the next plausible edit;

```
small, large = [], []
hash = collections.Counter()
balance = 0
for i in range(len(nums)):
    if not small or nums[i] <= -small[0]:
        heappush(small, -nums[i]); balance += 1
    else:
        heappush(large, nums[i]); balance -= 1
    # rebalance
    if balance > 0:
        heappush(large, -heappop(small)); balance -= 2
    elif balance < 0:
        heappush(small, -heappop(large)); balance += 2
    # remove outgoing
    if i >= k:
        out = nums[i-k]
        hash[out] += 1
        if out <= -small[0]:
            balance -= 1
        else:
            balance += 1
        # rebalance
        if balance > 0:
            heappush(large, -heappop(small)); balance -= 2
        elif balance < 0:
            heappush(small, -heappop(large)); balance += 2
        # clean
        while small and hash[-small[0]] > 0:
            hash[-small[0]] -= 1
            heappop(small)
        while large and hash[large[0]] > 0:
            hash[large[0]] -= 1
            heappop(large)
    if i >= k-1:
        if k % 2: ans.append(float(-small[0]))
        else: ans.append((-small[0] + large[0]) / 2.0)
```

Common solution:
```
def medianSlidingWindow(self, nums, k):
    lo, hi = [], []
    hash = collections.Counter()
    balance = 0
    for i in range(len(nums)):
        if not lo or nums[i] <= -lo[0]:
            heapq.heappush(lo, -nums[i])
            balance += 1
        else:
            heapq.heappush(hi, nums[i])
            balance -= 1
        if balance > 0:
            heapq.heappush(hi, -heapq.heappop(lo))
            balance -= 2
        elif balance < 0:
            heapq.heappush(lo, -heapq.heappop(hi))
            balance += 2
        if i >= k:
            out = nums[i-k]
            hash[out] += 1
            if out <= -lo[0]:
                balance -= 1
            else:
                balance += 1
            if balance > 0:
                heapq.heappush(hi, -heapq.heappop(lo))
                balance -= 2
            elif balance < 0:
                heapq.heappush(lo, -heapq.heappop(hi))
                balance += 2
            while lo and hash[-lo[0]]:
                hash[-lo[0]] -= 1
                heapq.heappop(lo)
            while hi and hash[hi[0]]:
                hash[hi[0]] -= 1
                heapq.heappop(hi)
        if i >= k-1:
            if k & 1:
                ans.append(float(-lo[0]))
            else:
                ans.append((-lo[0] + hi[0]) / 2.0)
```

Let's write code:

```
from typing import List
import heapq

class Solution:
    def medianSlidingWindow(self, nums: List[int], k: int) -> List[float]:
        small = []  # max heap, store (-num, index)
        large = []  # min heap, store (num, index)
        removed = [False] * len(nums)
        heap_of = [0] * len(nums)
        small_cnt = 0
        large_cnt = 0
        ans = []

        def clean_small():
            while small and removed[small[0][1]]:
                heapq.heappop(small)

        def clean_large():
            while large and removed[large[0][1]]:
                heapq.heappop(large)

        def rebalance():
            nonlocal small_cnt, large_cnt
            while small_cnt > large_cnt + 1:
                clean_small()
                neg_v, idx = heapq.heappop(small)
                small_cnt -= 1
                heapq.heappush(large, (-neg_v, idx))
                heap_of[idx] = 1
                large_cnt += 1
            while large_cnt > small_cnt:
                clean_large()
                v, idx = heapq.heappop(large)
                large_cnt -= 1
                heapq.heappush(small, (-v, idx))
                heap_of[idx] = 0
                small_cnt += 1

        for i, x in enumerate(nums):
            clean_small()
            clean_large()
            if not small or x <= -small[0][0]:
                heapq.heappush(small, (-x, i))
                small_cnt += 1
                heap_of[i] = 0
            else:
                heapq.heappush(large, (x, i))
                large_cnt += 1
                heap_of[i] = 1

            rebalance()

            if i >= k:
                idx = i - k
                removed[idx] = True
                if heap_of[idx] == 0:
                    small_cnt -= 1
                else:
                    large_cnt -= 1
                rebalance()

            if i >= k - 1:
                clean_small()
                clean_large()
                if k % 2 == 1:
                    ans.append(float(-small[0][0]))
                else:
                    ans.append((-small[0][0] + large[0][0]) / 2.0)

        return ans
```

Let's think if there is a possible issue with `nonlocal small_cnt, large_cnt` if the nested function `rebalance` is defined before `small_cnt` and `large_cnt` are assigned? In Python, nonlocal requires binding in enclosing scope; assignments occur before function definition? Actually in code:
```
small_cnt = 0
large_cnt = 0
def rebalance():
    nonlocal small_cnt, large_cnt
```
The variables are bound in enclosing scope before def. Good. If we defined rebalance before assignments, nonlocal would fail. We'll order assignments before def. Good.

Potential issue: If `nums` contains `tuple
</think>

=== RAW ANSWER ===
```python
from typing import List
import heapq

class Solution:
    def medianSlidingWindow(self, nums: List[int], k: int) -> List[float]:
        small = []
        large = []
        removed = [False] * len(nums)
        heap_of = [0] * len(nums)
        small_cnt = 0
        large_cnt = 0
        ans = []

        def clean_small():
            while small and removed[small[0][1]]:
                heapq.heappop(small)

        def clean_large():
            while large and removed[large[0][1]]:
                heapq.heappop(large)

        def rebalance():
            nonlocal small_cnt, large_cnt

            while small_cnt > large_cnt + 1:
                clean_small()
                neg_v, idx = heapq.heappop(small)
                small_cnt -= 1
                heapq.heappush(large, (-neg_v, idx))
                heap_of[idx] = 1
                large_cnt += 1

            while large_cnt > small_cnt:
                clean_large()
                v, idx = heapq.heappop(large)
                large_cnt -= 1
                heapq.heappush(small, (-v, idx))
                heap_of[idx] = 0
                small_cnt += 1

        for i, x in enumerate(nums):
            clean_small()
            clean_large()

            if not small or x <= -small[0][0]:
                heapq.heappush(small, (-x, i))
                small_cnt += 1
                heap_of[i] = 0
            else:
                heapq.heappush(large, (x, i))
                large_cnt += 1
                heap_of[i] = 1

            rebalance()

            if i >= k:
                idx = i - k
                removed[idx] = True
                if heap_of[idx] == 0:
                    small_cnt -= 1
                else:
                    large_cnt -= 1
                rebalance()

            if i >= k - 1:
                clean_small()
                clean_large()

                if k % 2 == 1:
                    ans.append(float(-small[0][0]))
                else:
                    ans.append((-small[0][0] + large[0][0]) / 2.0)

        return ans
```

=== FIXED CODE ===
from typing import List
import heapq

class Solution:
    def medianSlidingWindow(self, nums: List[int], k: int) -> List[float]:
        small = []
        large = []
        removed = [False] * len(nums)
        heap_of = [0] * len(nums)
        small_cnt = 0
        large_cnt = 0
        ans = []

        def clean_small():
            while small and removed[small[0][1]]:
                heapq.heappop(small)

        def clean_large():
            while large and removed[large[0][1]]:
                heapq.heappop(large)

        def rebalance():
            nonlocal small_cnt, large_cnt

            while small_cnt > large_cnt + 1:
                clean_small()
                neg_v, idx = heapq.heappop(small)
                small_cnt -= 1
                heapq.heappush(large, (-neg_v, idx))
                heap_of[idx] = 1
                large_cnt += 1

            while large_cnt > small_cnt:
                clean_large()
                v, idx = heapq.heappop(large)
                large_cnt -= 1
                heapq.heappush(small, (-v, idx))
                heap_of[idx] = 0
                small_cnt += 1

        for i, x in enumerate(nums):
            clean_small()
            clean_large()

            if not small or x <= -small[0][0]:
                heapq.heappush(small, (-x, i))
                small_cnt += 1
                heap_of[i] = 0
            else:
                heapq.heappush(large, (x, i))
                large_cnt += 1
                heap_of[i] = 1

            rebalance()

            if i >= k:
                idx = i - k
                removed[idx] = True
                if heap_of[idx] == 0:
                    small_cnt -= 1
                else:
                    large_cnt -= 1
                rebalance()

            if i >= k - 1:
                clean_small()
                clean_large()

                if k % 2 == 1:
                    ans.append(float(-small[0][0]))
                else:
                    ans.append((-small[0][0] + large[0][0]) / 2.0)

        return ans
